# Awesome LLM4OR

**English** | [简体中文](README.zh-CN.md)

A curated collection of papers, benchmarks, datasets, models, and tools for **Large Language Models for Operations Research**.

Research on LLMs for operations research, covering mathematical modeling, solver code, algorithm design, agents, training, and evaluation. Paper, code, model, and data links are listed on this page; Details opens the corresponding author, version, and resource notes below.

**Updated: 2026-10-25 · 235 papers.** A paper may appear in multiple modules; the total counts unique paper IDs.

**Reading the tables:** Contribution labels such as Method, Benchmark, Data, and Model describe what a paper introduces and may overlap. For example, ORLM contributes a training method, IndustryOR, data, and domain models, so it appears in modeling, training, and benchmark modules. Resource columns show confirmed links, with third-party mirrors and related resources labeled.

**Reading tiers:** Core marks representative methods, foundational work, or important evaluation resources; Exploratory marks adjacent research such as open-ended mathematical modeling. Other entries are supplementary. Tiers guide reading rather than rank paper quality; see [Classification guide](#classification).

[Authors and versions](#paper-info) · [Classification guide](#classification)

<a id="modeling"></a>

## Problem understanding and mathematical modeling

Extracting variables, objectives, and constraints from descriptions to formulate LP, MILP, CP, and other mathematical models.

| Year / Venue | Paper | Contribution summary | Contributions | Resources |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| 2026 · arXiv preprint | **Agora-Opt**<br>From Soliloquy to Agora: Memory-Enhanced LLM Agents with Decentralized Debate for Optimization Modeling | Uses independent modeling teams and outcome-driven debate, with read/write memory to reuse solving experience and revisions arising from disagreements. | Method · Data | [![Paper][b049]][r103] · [![Code & Cleaned Data][b050]][r083] · [Details](#paper-soliloquy2026) |
| 2026 · ICML 2026 RLxF Workshop / arXiv | **AutoOR**<br>AutoOR: Scalably Post-training LLMs to Autoformulate Operations Research Problems | Synthesizes training examples from standard optimization forms and trains natural-language modeling through execution feedback and curriculum RL. | Method · Data · Model | [![Paper 1][b051]][r104] · [![Paper 2][b052]][r105] · [Details](#paper-autoor2026) |
| 2026 · arXiv preprint | **AutoREM / AutoRO-Bench**<br>Automated Reformulation of Robust Optimization via Memory-Augmented Large Language Models | Uses failure-experience memory for deterministic reformulation of robust optimization and introduces AutoRO-Bench. | Method · Benchmark · Data | [![Paper][b049]][r106] · [Details](#paper-robustreform2026) |
| 2026 · LION 19 / LNCS 15745 (published 2026) | **CHORUS**<br>CHORUS: Zero-shot Hierarchical Retrieval and Orchestration for Generating Linear Programming Code | Supplies knowledge for Gurobi LP code generation through hierarchical document chunking, metadata retrieval, and reranking. | Method · Benchmark · Data | [![Publication][b053]][r107] · [![Paper 2][b052]][r108] · [Details](#paper-lporch2025) |
| 2026 · arXiv preprint | **CIR / R2C**<br>Canonical Intermediate Representation for LLM-based optimization problem formulation and code generation | Encodes complex rules in a canonical intermediate representation before retrieving knowledge and generating constraint implementations. | Method · Benchmark · Data | [![Paper][b049]][r109] · [Details](#paper-canonicalir2026) |
| 2026 · ACM Computing Surveys 58 (2026) | **Combinatorial Optimization Systematic Review**<br>Large Language Models for Combinatorial Optimization: A Systematic Review | Uses a PRISMA review process to organize LLM combinatorial optimization studies by tasks, architectures, evaluation data, and application domains. | Survey | [![Paper 1][b051]][r110] · [![Paper 2][b052]][r111] · [Details](#paper-lco2026review) |
| 2026 · arXiv preprint | **COOPA**<br>COOPA: A Modular LLM Agent Architecture for Operations Research Problems | Iteratively compares formulation confidence, traces modeling-element provenance, and routes tasks to suitable solver agents. | Method | [![Paper][b049]][r112] · [![Code][b022]][r113] · [Details](#paper-coopa2026) |
| 2026 · LLM4Code 2026 Workshop @ ICSE | **CP-Agent**<br>CP-Agent: Agentic Constraint Programming | Executes and iteratively repairs constraint models with a persistent IPython kernel and concise domain prompts. | Method · Data | [![Paper 1][b051]][r114] · [![Paper 2][b052]][r115] · [![Author Coding Agent (related)][b054]][r116] · [![Evaluation Archive v2][b055]][r087] · [Details](#paper-cpagent2025) |
| 2026 · arXiv preprint | **CP-SynC**<br>CP-SynC: Multi-Agent Zero-Shot Constraint Modeling in MiniZinc with Synthesized Checkers | Uses modeling, checking, and selection agents to explore MiniZinc models in parallel and synthesizes semantic checkers to aggregate evidence. | Method · Benchmark · Data | [![Paper][b049]][r117] · [![Benchmark & Evaluation][b056]][r028] · [Details](#paper-cpsync2026) |
| 2026 · AAAI 2026 | **DeepOR**<br>DeepOR: A Deep Reasoning Foundation Model for Optimization Modeling | Uses flowcharts to guide long-chain-of-thought synthesis and SFT, followed by RL with solver-feedback rewards. | Method · Data | [![Paper][b057]][r118] · [![Code][b022]][r119] · [![Official seeds and sample data][b028]][r072] · [Details](#paper-xiao2026deepor) |
| 2026 · arXiv preprint | **EVOM**<br>Execution-Verified Reinforcement Learning for Optimization Modeling | Trains modeling LLMs with solver execution outcomes and studies transfer across Gurobi, OR-Tools, and COPT. | Method · Model | [![Paper][b049]][r120] · [Details](#paper-guan2026evom) |
| 2026 · arXiv preprint | **EvoOpt-LLM**<br>EvoOpt-LLM: Evolving industrial optimization models with large language models | Uses a 7B model and LoRA for industrial MILP modeling, new business-constraint injection, and variable pruning. | Method · Model | [![Paper][b049]][r121] · [![Code][b022]][r122] · [Details](#paper-he2026evoopt) |
| 2026 · NeurIPS 2026 (accepted; publication pending) | **EvoOptiGraph**<br>EvoOptiGraph: Weakness-Driven Coevolution via Graph-Based Structural Generation for Optimization Modeling | Evolves structurally diverse MILPs as attributed bipartite graphs, using compilation, back-translation, SFT, and RLVR to target model weaknesses. | Method · Data · Model | [![Paper 1][b051]][r123] · [![Paper 2][b052]][r124] · [Details](#paper-evooptigraph2026) |
| 2026 · arXiv preprint | **EvoOR-Agent**<br>Co-evolving Agent Architectures and Interpretable Reasoning for Automated Optimization | Represents modeling–solving workflows as AOE graphs and co-evolves agent architectures with interpretable reasoning paths. | Method | [![Paper][b049]][r125] · [Details](#paper-coevolve2026) |
| 2026 · arXiv; NeurIPS 2026 workshop version | **Formalize, Don't Optimize**<br>Formalize, Don't Optimize: The Heuristic Trap in LLM-Generated Combinatorial Solvers | Uses CP-SynC-XL to compare native search, Python solver APIs, and MiniZinc, auditing errors introduced by search optimization. | Benchmark · Data | [![Paper 1][b058]][r126] · [![Workshop][b059]][r127] · [Details](#paper-wang2026formalize) |
| 2026 · EMNLP 2026 | **FormuEvo**<br>FormuEvo: LLM-Guided Evolution for Discovering Solver-Efficient Mixed-Integer Programming Formulations | Evolves stronger MIP formulations in modeling-program space, guiding rewrites with solver-statistics diagnostics and structured memory. | Method | [![Publication][b053]][r128] · [![Paper 2][b052]][r129] · [![Code][b022]][r130] · [Details](#paper-yuan2026formuevo) |
| 2026 · NeurIPS 2026 (accepted; publication pending) | **FrontierOR**<br>FrontierOR: Benchmarking LLMs' Capacity for Efficient Algorithm Design in Large-Scale Optimization | Evaluates LLMs on generating efficient, feasible algorithms for large-scale, literature-driven OR tasks. | Benchmark · Data | [![Paper 1][b051]][r131] · [![Paper 2][b052]][r132] · [![Code][b022]][r034] · [![Dataset][b060]][r033] · [Details](#paper-kong2026frontieror) |
| 2026 · arXiv preprint | **Interactive Optimization Conversations**<br>Let's Have a Conversation: Designing and Evaluating LLM Agents for Interactive Optimization | Uses decision agents with internal utility functions as stakeholders to evaluate multi-turn interactive optimization beyond single-answer quality. | Method · Data · Application | [![Paper][b049]][r133] · [![Code][b022]][r090] · [Details](#paper-conversation2026) |
| 2026 · arXiv preprint | **LEAN-LLM-OPT**<br>Large-Scale Optimization Model Auto-Formulation: Harnessing LLM Flexibility via Structured Workflow | Builds modeling workflows dynamically and delegates data processing to tools for large-scale optimization and airline revenue management. | Method · Benchmark · Data · Application | [![Paper][b049]][r134] · [![Code][b022]][r038] · [Details](#paper-liang2026leanllmopt) |
| 2026 · IEEE CIM (journal record) | **LLMs for Evolutionary Optimization Survey**<br>A Systematic Survey on Large Language Models for Evolutionary Optimization: From Modeling to Solving | Surveys LLMs and evolutionary optimization from modeling to solving, distinguishing direct optimization, internal assistance, and high-level algorithm design. | Survey | [![Journal record][b061]][r135] · [![Paper 2][b052]][r136] · [![Literature Collection (related)][b062]][r137] · [Details](#paper-sysevo2025) |
| 2026 · arXiv preprint | **MIND**<br>Automated Optimization Modeling via a Localizable Error-Driven Perspective | Synthesizes targeted training tasks from localized modeling errors and applies DFPO to locally revise difficult examples. | Method · Data · Model | [![Paper][b049]][r138] · [Details](#paper-mind2026) |

## Benchmarks, datasets, and evaluation

Introducing benchmarks, datasets, evaluation methods, or systematic evaluation analyses. Methods evaluated only on existing benchmarks are not included in this module for that reason alone.

<a id="applications"></a>

## OR applications

Supply chains, inventory, routing, scheduling, production, and other optimization applications.

### Scheduling and production

| Year / Venue | Paper | Contribution summary | Contributions | Resources |
|---|---|---|---|---|

<a id="contributing"></a>

## Scope and contributions

Contributions through Issues or Pull Requests are welcome. Please provide the title, authors, year, publication status, paper link, a concise contribution summary, and confirmed resource links. Each work has one stable ID, with related versions recorded together. Papers may belong to multiple modules; see the [Classification guide](#classification).

- **Scope:** Prioritizes optimization modeling, solvers, OR algorithms, and relevant evaluation; adjacent research is explicitly labeled.
- **Accuracy:** Distinguishes publication, author-reported acceptance, preprints, and workshops. A resource's existence does not establish an official release. Checks cover metadata, method summaries, and link-level evidence; they do not imply experimental reproduction.
- **Citations and versions:** See this page's [paper information](#paper-info) for authors, related versions, and resource scope. Cite formal paper records or original citations with explicit version information.

Related collections: [LLM4OR][r238] · [awesome-fm4co][r564] · [LLM4AlgorithmDesign][r423]. This collection checks entries individually and applies its own multi-label taxonomy; inclusion in external lists alone does not establish verification.

<a id="paper-info"></a>

## Authors, versions, and resource notes

Records follow formal publications or specified preprints. Each work retains one ID; authors, titles, and experimental scope may differ between related versions.

| Paper and authors | Version and resource notes |
|---|---|

High experimental feasibility does not guarantee correctness for arbitrary new constraints. |

| <a id="paper-ramamonjison2022augmenting"></a>**Auto-formulation (2022)**<br>Rindra Ramamonjison; Haley Li; Timothy Yu; Shiqi He; Vishnu Rengan; Amin Banitalebi-Dehkordi; Zirui Zhou; Yong Zhang | The original PDF's first page links NL4Opt competition code and data. The 1,101 examples split into 713/99/289 train/development/test cases. Shares data lineage with the later competition report; papers retain separate IDs without counting two independent datasets.<br>Code & Data (NL4Opt): The paper's first page links shared competition code and data. generation_data, ner_data, and evaluation scripts are available; a complete interactive UI is not confirmed. |

[Back to contents](#contents)

<a id="classification"></a>

## Classification guide

Each paper has one primary category and all relevant module labels based on its actual contributions. Cross-module appearances do not increase the paper count.

- **Core / Supplementary / Exploratory:** Core entries represent major research directions, foundational methods, or important evaluation resources. Supplementary entries cover specific methods and settings; exploratory entries identify adjacent work's OR relevance. Tiers guide reading rather than rank quality.
- **Methods and benchmarks:** Papers introducing both a method and a new benchmark receive both labels. Evaluating a method on existing benchmarks does not introduce a benchmark.
- **Data and models:** Training data, evaluation benchmarks, revised evaluation data, and models are listed separately. Introducing a model does not imply public weights; reusing data does not introduce the dataset.
- **Training and program evolution:** LLM weight updates are distinguished from generating, selecting, or evolving algorithm programs. Auxiliary-network or NCO-policy training is not automatically LLM fine-tuning.
- **Verification:** Executability, feasibility, matching objectives, graph isomorphism, mathematical equivalence, and fidelity to problem intent are distinguished. Behavioral perturbation diagnostics are not complete correctness proofs.
- **Resources:** Third-party reproductions and related resources are labeled; resource links reflect the artifacts actually released by each paper.
